event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { FC, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ChevronLeft,\n  ChevronRight,\n  Shuffle,\n  ListFilter,\n  Check,\n  RotateCcw,\n  BookOpen\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordItem, CategoryType } from './types';\nimport { defaultWords } from './data/defaultWords';\nimport FlashCard from './components/FlashCard';\nimport WordListModal from './components/WordListModal';\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<WordItem[]>(() => {\n    return defaultWords;\n  });\n  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<CategoryType>('all');\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // カテゴリで絞り込み\n  const filteredWords = useMemo(() => {\n    if (selectedCategory === 'all') {\n      return words;\n    }\n    return words.filter((w) => w.category === selectedCategory);\n  }, [words, selectedCategory]);\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentWord = filteredWords[currentIndex] || filteredWords[0] || null;\n\n  // 習得進捗\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => {\n    return filteredWords.filter((w) => w.isMastered).length;\n  }, [filteredWords]);\n\n  const progressPercent = useMemo(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length === 0) return 0;\n    return Math.round((masteredCount / filteredWords.length) * 100);\n  }, [masteredCount, filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length <= 1) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : filteredWords.length - 1));\n  }, [filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length <= 1) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev < filteredWords.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : 0));\n  }, [filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // カードを反転\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 覚えた状態の切り替え\n  const handleToggleMaster = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    setWords((prevWords) =>\n      prevWords.map((w) =>\n        w.id === id ? { ...w, isMastered: !w.isMastered } : w\n      )\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  // カテゴリ変更\n  const handleCategoryChange = (cat: CategoryType) => {\n    setSelectedCategory(cat);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // シャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setWords((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n  };\n\n  // 単語追加\n  const handleAddWord = (newWordData: Omit<WordItem, 'id' | 'isMastered'>) => {\n    const newItem: WordItem = {\n      ...newWordData,\n      id: `w-${Date.now()}`,\n      isMastered: false\n    };\n    setWords((prev) => [newItem, ...prev]);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // 単語削除\n  const handleDeleteWord = (id: string) => {\n    setWords((prev) => prev.filter((w) => w.id !== id));\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // モーダルから単語選択\n  const handleSelectWord = (id: string) => {\n    const targetIdx = filteredWords.findIndex((w) => w.id === id);\n    if (targetIdx !== -1) {\n      setCurrentIndex(targetIdx);\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n    } else {\n      setSelectedCategory('all');\n      const allIdx = words.findIndex((w) => w.id === id);\n      if (allIdx !== -1) {\n        setCurrentIndex(allIdx);\n        setIsFlipped(false);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isModalOpen) return;\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement || e.target instanceof HTMLSelectElement) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n        handleNext();\n      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        handlePrev();\n      } else if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [handleNext, handlePrev, handleFlip, isModalOpen]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* ヘッダーエリア */}\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title\">\n          <span>FlashVocab 3D</span>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>\n            <BookOpen size={18} color=\"#818cf8\" />\n            <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>英単語学習</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 進捗メーター */}\n        <div className=\"header-stats\">\n          <div className=\"progress-text\">\n            <span>\n              暗記進捗: {masteredCount} / {filteredWords.length} 語\n            </span>\n            <span>{progressPercent}% 完了</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"progress-bar-container\">\n            <div\n              className=\"progress-bar-fill\"\n              style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* カテゴリ選択タブ */}\n        <div className=\"category-tabs\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'all' ? 'category-btn category-btn-active' : 'category-btn'}\n            onClick={() => handleCategoryChange('all')}\n          >\n            すべて ({words.length})\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'daily' ? 'category-btn category-btn-active' : 'category-btn'}\n            onClick={() => handleCategoryChange('daily')}\n          >\n            日常会話\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'business' ? 'category-btn category-btn-active' : 'category-btn'}\n            onClick={() => handleCategoryChange('business')}\n          >\n            ビジネス\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'toeic' ? 'category-btn category-btn-active' : 'category-btn'}\n            onClick={() => handleCategoryChange('toeic')}\n          >\n            TOEIC\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* メインカード表示 */}\n      {currentWord ? (\n        <FlashCard\n          word={currentWord}\n          isFlipped={isFlipped}\n          onFlip={handleFlip}\n          onToggleMaster={handleToggleMaster}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"empty-state\">\n          <h3>単語が見つかりません</h3>\n          <p>「単語一覧」から新しい単語を追加するか、他のカテゴリを選択してください。</p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* ナビゲーション操作エリア */}\n      <div>\n        <div className=\"nav-controls\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"nav-btn btn-prev\"\n            onClick={handlePrev}\n            disabled={filteredWords.length <= 1}\n            aria-label=\"前の単語へ\"\n          >\n            <ChevronLeft size={18} /> 前へ\n          </button>\n\n          {currentWord && (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={currentWord.isMastered ? 'nav-btn btn-review' : 'nav-btn btn-master'}\n              onClick={() => handleToggleMaster(currentWord.id)}\n            >\n              {currentWord.isMastered ? (\n                <>\n                  <RotateCcw size={18} /> 復習に戻す\n                </>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <Check size={18} /> 覚えた！\n                </>\n              )}\n            </button>\n          )}\n\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"nav-btn btn-next\"\n            onClick={handleNext}\n            disabled={filteredWords.length <= 1}\n            aria-label=\"次の単語へ\"\n          >\n            次へ <ChevronRight size={18} />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 補助アクションボタン */}\n        <div className=\"action-panel\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"action-btn\"\n            onClick={handleShuffle}\n            title=\"カードをランダムに並び替え\"\n          >\n            <Shuffle size={16} /> シャッフル\n          </button>\n\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"action-btn\"\n            onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}\n            title=\"全単語の確認と新規追加\"\n          >\n            <ListFilter size={16} /> 単語一覧・追加\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 単語一覧・追加モーダル */}\n      <WordListModal\n        isOpen={isModalOpen}\n        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}\n        words={words}\n        currentWordId={currentWord ? currentWord.id : ''}\n        onSelectWord={handleSelectWord}\n        onAddWord={handleAddWord}\n        onDeleteWord={handleDeleteWord}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [isOpen, onClose]);\n\n  if (!isOpen) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const handleOverlayClick = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) =>    setErrorMessage('英単語と日本語訳は必須項目です。');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    onAddWord?.({\n      word: newWord.trim(),\n      phonetic: newPhonetic.trim() || `/${newWord.trim().toLowerCase()}/`,\n ListFilter,\n  Check,\n  RotateCcw,\n  BookOpen\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordItem, CategoryType } from './types';\nimport { defaultWords } from './data/defaultWords';\nimport FlashCard from './components/FlashCard';\nimport WordListModal from './components/WordListModal';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'flashvocab_words_data';\n\nconst generateUniqueId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return `w-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n  }\n  return `w-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<WordItem[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (saved) {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {\n          return parsed;\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to load words from localStorage:', e);\n    }\n    return defaultWords;\n  });\n\n  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<CategoryType>('all');\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // localStorage への永続化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(words));\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to save words to localStorage:', e);\n    }\n  }, [words]);\n\n  // カテゴリで絞り込み\n  const filteredWords = useMemo(() => {\n    if (selectedCategory === 'all') {\n      return words;\n    }\n    return words.filter((w) => w.category === selectedCategory);\n  }, [words, selectedCategory]);\n\n  // filteredWords の長さ変化時に currentIndex を安全な範囲内にクランプ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length === 0) {\n      if (currentIndex !== 0) {\n        setCurrentIndex(0);\n      }\n    } else if (currentIndex >= filteredWords.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(filteredWords.length - 1);\n    }\n  }, [filteredWords.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentWord =\n    filteredWords.length > 0 && currentIndex < filteredWords.length\n      ? filteredWords[currentIndex]\n      : null;\n\n  // 習得進捗\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => {\n    return filteredWords.filter((w) => w.isMastered).length;\n  }, [filteredWords]);\n\n  const progressPercent = useMemo(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length === 0) return 0;\n    return Math.round((masteredCount / filteredWords.length) * 100);\n  }, [masteredCount, filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length <= 1) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : filteredWords.length - 1));\n  }, [filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length <= 1) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev < filteredWords.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : 0));\n  }, [filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // カードを反転\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 覚えた状態の切り替え\n  const handleToggleMaster = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    setWords((prevWords) =>\n      prevWords.map((w) =>\n        w.id === id ? { ...w, isMastered: !w.isMastered } : w\n      )\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  // カテゴリ変更\n  const handleCategoryChange = (cat: CategoryType) => {\n    setSelectedCategory(cat);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // シャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setWords((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n  };\n\n  // 単語追加\n  const handleAddWord = (newWordData: Omit<WordItem, 'id' | 'isMastered'>) => {\n    const newItem: WordItem = {\n      ...newWordData,\n      id: generateUniqueId(),\n      isMastered: false\n    };\n    setWords((prev) => [newItem, ...prev]);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // 単語削除\n  const handleDeleteWord = (id: string) => {\n    setWords((prev) => prev.filter((w) => w.id !== id));\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // モーダルから単語選択\n  const handleSelectWord = (id: string) => {\n    const targetIdx = filteredWords.findIndex((w) => w.id === id);\n    if (targetIdx !== -1) {\n      setCurrentIndex(targetIdx);\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n    } else {\n      setSelectedCategory('all');\n      const allIdx = words.findIndex((w) => w.id === id);\n      if (allIdx !== -1) {\n        setCurrentIndex(allIdx);\n        setIsFlipped(false);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isModalOpen) return;\n      if (\n        e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement ||\n        e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement ||\n        e.target instanceof HTMLSelectElement\n      ) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n        handleNext();\n      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        handlePrev();\n      } else if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [handleNext, handlePrev, handleFlip, isModalOpen]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* ヘッダーエリア */}\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title\">\n          <span>FlashVocab 3D</span>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>\n            <BookOpen size={18} color=\"#818cf8\" />\n            <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>英単語学習</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 進捗メーター */}\n        <div className=\"header-stats\">\n          <div className=\"progress-text\">\n            <span>\n              暗記進捗: {masteredCount} / {filteredWords.length} 語\n            </span>\n            <span>{progressPercent}% 完了</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"progress-bar-container\">\n            <div\n              className=\"progress-bar-fill\"\n              style={{ (\n        <FlashCard\n          word={currentWord}\n          isFlipped={isFlipped}\n          onFlip={handleFlip}\n          onToggleMaster={handleToggleMaster}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"empty-state\">\n          <h3>単語が見つかりません</h3>\n          <p>「単語一覧」から新しい単語を追加するか、他のカテゴリを選択してください。</p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* ナビゲーション操作エリア */}\n      <div>\n        <div className=\"nav-controls\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"nav-btn btn-prev\"\n            onClick={handlePrev}\n            disabled={filteredWords.length <= 1}\n            aria-label=\"前の単語へ\"\n          >\n            <ChevronLeft size={18} /> 前へ\n          </button>\n\n          {currentWord && (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={currentWord.isMastered ? MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, textToSpeak: string) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {\n      alert('お使いのブラウザは音声読み上げに対応していません。');\n      return;\n    }\n\n utterance.onerror = (event) => {\n        console.error('Speech synthesis error:', event);\n      };\n      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('音声再生に失敗しました:', error);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onFlip?.();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleClick = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    if (word) {\n      onToggleMaster?.(word.id);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if  onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}\n        tabIndex={0}\n        role=\"button\"\n        aria-label={`単語カード: ${word.word}。クリックまたはスペースキーで裏返します`}\n      >\n        <div className={isFlipped ? window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [isOpen, onClose]);\n\n  if (!isOpen) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const handleOverlayClick = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) =>    setErrorMessage('英単語と日本語訳は必須項目です。');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    onAddWord?.({\n      word: newWord.trim(),\n      phonetic: newPhonetic.trim() || `/${newWord.trim().toLowerCase()}/`,\n    >\n                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '2px', overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' {item.word}\n                    </strong>\n                    <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', color: '#64748b' }}>({item.partOfSpeech})</span>\n                    {item.isMastered && (\n     )}\n                  </div>\n                  <div style={{ fontSize: '0.85rem', color: '#475569', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', overflow: 'hidden', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>\n })}\n        </div>\n\n        <form className=\"add-word-form\" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: '0.95rem', fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', display: 'flex', alignItems: }}>\n              {errorMessage}\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '10px' }}>\n            <div ListFilter,\n  Check,\n  RotateCcw,\n  BookOpen\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordItem, CategoryType } from './types';\nimport { defaultWords } from './data/defaultWords';\nimport FlashCard from './components/FlashCard';\nimport WordListModal from './components/WordListModal';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'flashvocab_words_data';\n\nconst generateUniqueId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return `w-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n  }\n  return `w-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<WordItem[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (saved) {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {\n          return parsed;\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to load words from localStorage:', e);\n    }\n    return defaultWords;\n  });\n\n  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<CategoryType>('all');\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // localStorage への永続化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(words));\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to save words to localStorage:', e);\n    }\n  }, [words]);\n\n  // カテゴリで絞り込み\n  const filteredWords = useMemo(() => {\n    if (selectedCategory === 'all') {\n      return words;\n    }\n    return words.filter((w) => w.category === selectedCategory);\n  }, [words, selectedCategory]);\n\n  // filteredWords の長さ変化時に currentIndex を安全な範囲内にクランプ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filteredWords.length === 0) {\n      if (currentIndex !== 0) {\n        setCurrentIndex(0);\n      }\n    } else if (currentIndex >= filteredWords.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(filteredWords.length - 1);\n    }\n  }, [filteredWords.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentWord =\n    filteredWords.length > 0 && currentIndex < filteredWords.length\n      ? filteredWords[currentIndex]\n      : null;\n\n  // 習得進捗\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => {\n    return => {\n    if (filteredWords.length <= 1) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev < filteredWords.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : 0));\n  }, [filteredWords.length]);\n\n  // カードを反転\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 覚えた状態の切り替え\n  const handleToggleMaster = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    setWords((prevWords) =>\n      prevWords.map((w) =>\n        w.id === id ? { ...w, isMastered: !w.isMastered } : w\n      )\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  // カテゴリ変更\n  const handleCategoryChange = (cat: CategoryType) => {\n    setSelectedCategory(cat);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // シャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setWords((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n string) => {\n    setWords((prev) => prev.filter((w) => w.id !== id));\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // モーダルから単語選択\n  const handleSelectWord = (id: string) => {\n    const targetIdx =       e.target instanceof HTMLSelectElement\n      ) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n        handleNext();\n      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        handlePrev();\n      } else if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n */}\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title\">\n          <span>FlashVocab 3D</span>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px',   <span>{progressPercent}% 完了</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"progress-bar-container\">\n            <div\n              className=\"progress-bar-fill\"\n              style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* カテゴリ選択タブ */}\n        <div className=\"category-tabs\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'all' ? 'category-btn category-btn-active' : 'category-btn'}\n            onClick={() => handleCategoryChange('toeic')}\n          >\n            TOEIC\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* メインカード表示 */}\n      {currentWord ?
